# Java Audiveris 5.11 (Temurin JDK 25) BlackHeadSizer oracle.
#
# Every page reaches the real STEM_SEEDS step in a fresh JVM, then
# production SpotsBuilder.buildSpots performs its BEAMS raster work and
# invokes BlackHeadSizer.process. HEAD_SPOTS is the threshold-170 table;
# BlackHeadSizer consumes the separately listed threshold-140 components.
#
# Pixel digests are FNV-1a-64 in row-major byte order. Run-table and row
# digests are FNV-1a-64 over UTF-8 canonical records with trailing newline.
# Components, singles and stacks retain source order. Samples use Java's
# stable width sort, and core=true names the [n/4, 3n/4) middle half.
# Population and scale doubles use Double.toHexString.
#
# Compile and run from app/ with the frozen JDK and saved runtime classpath.
# Generate one target per fresh JVM, in this order: chula, allegretto,
# batuque, carmen, cucaracha, hove, zizi, BachInvention5.
